<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A film starts at $6{:}45 \ \text{pm}$ and lasts for $2 \ \text{hours } 35 \ \text{minutes}$.

At what time will it finish?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A film starts at $6{:}45 \ \text{pm}$ and lasts for $2 \ \text{hours } 35 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>
At what time will it finish?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{End time} &= 6{:}45  + 2 \ \text{hours } 35 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                 &= 9{:}20 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9{:}20 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A coach left London Victoria $5 \ \text{minutes}$ late, at $13{:}45$, 
and arrived in Banbury $14 \ \text{minutes}$ early, at $17{:}05$. 
How long should the journey have taken if the coach had left and arrived on time?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A coach left London Victoria $5 \ \text{minutes}$ late, at $13{:}45$, 
and arrived in Banbury $14 \ \text{minutes}$ early, at $17{:}05$. 
How long should the journey have taken if the coach had left and arrived on time?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Original time to leave}    &= 13{:}45 - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                  &= 13{:}40  \\\\
\text {Original time to arrive}   &= 17{:}05 + 14 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                  &= 17{:}19 \\\\
\text {Duration of journey}       &= 17{:}19 - 13{:}40 \\\\
                                  &= 3 \ \text {hours} \ 39 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \ \text {hours} \ 39 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

                                  &= 17{:}19 \\\\

                                  &= 3 \ \text {hours} \ 39 \ \text{minutes}

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Chris runs round a track at a speed of $6 \ \text{km/hour}$.

Dave runs round the same track at a speed of $9 \ \text{km/hour}$.

When Chris has run $18$ laps, how many laps has Dave run?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Chris runs round a track at a speed of $6 \ \text{km/hour}$.
<emptyline>
Dave runs round the same track at a speed of $9 \ \text{km/hour}$.
<emptyline>
When Chris has run $18$ laps, how many laps has Dave run?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Ratio of their speed}            &= \dfrac {9} {6} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 3} {\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
\text {Laps ran by Dave}                &= \dfrac {3} {2} \ \text {of} \ 18 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3} {2} \times 18 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 18} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                        &= 3 \times 9 \\\\
                                        &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>

                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 3} {\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
\text {Laps ran by Dave}                &= \dfrac {3} {2} \ \text {of} \ 18 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3} {2} \times 18 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 18} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 9 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                        &= 3 \times 9 \\\\
                                        &= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My train was scheduled to leave at $17{:}40$ and to arrive at $18{:}20$. However it started $5  \ \text{minutes}$ late and the journey then took $42  \ \text{minutes}$. At what time did I arrive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My train was scheduled to leave at $17{:}40$ and to arrive at $18{:}20$. However it started $5  \ \text{minutes}$ late and the journey then took $42  \ \text{minutes}$. At what time did I arrive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival Time}     &= 17{:}40 + 5 \ \text{minutes} + 42 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 17{:}40 + 47 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 18{:}27
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18{:}27$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

How many seconds are there in $\dfrac {1} {4}$ of $\dfrac {1} {6}$ of $\dfrac {1} {8}$ of a day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds are there in $\dfrac {1} {4}$ of $\dfrac {1} {6}$ of $\dfrac {1} {8}$ of a day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text {of} \ \dfrac {1} {8} \ \text {of} \ 1 \ \text {day} \\\\ 
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {1} {6} \times \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \ \text {hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times  \dfrac {1} {6} \times  \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \times 60 \times 60  \ \text {seconds}\\\\\
&= \dfrac {24 \times 60 \times 60} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 4 \times 15 \times 6 \times 10} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times \cancel {4} \times 15 \times \cancel {6} \times 10} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 15 \times 10 \\\\
&= 45 \times 10 \\\\
&= 450 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$450 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {1} {6} \times \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \ \text {hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times  \dfrac {1} {6} \times  \dfrac {1} {8} \times 24 \times 60 \times 60  \ \text {seconds}\\\\\
&= \dfrac {24 \times 60 \times 60} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 4 \times 15 \times 6 \times 10} {4 \times 6 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 3 \times \cancel {4} \times 15 \times \cancel {6} \times 10} {\cancel {4} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 15 \times 10 \\\\
&= 45 \times 10 \\\\
&= 450 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There are $329$ days until my birthday. How many weeks is this? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There are $329$ days until my birthday. How many weeks is this? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Weeks until birthday} &= 329 \ \text{days} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {329} {7} \ \text {weeks}\\\\
                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 47 } {7} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 7 \times 47 } {\cancel 7} \\\\
                            &=47 \ \text {weeks}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$47 \ \text {weeks}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train leaves Solihull at $6.35 \ \text{am}$ and arrives at Liverpool at $10.22 \ \text{am}$. How many minutes does it take? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train leaves Solihull at $6.35 \ \text{am}$ and arrives at Liverpool at $10.22 \ \text{am}$. How many minutes does it take? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 10{:}22 \ \text{am} - 6{:}35 \ \text{am} \\\\
                      &= 3 \ \text{hours} \ 47 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 3 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 47 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 180 + 47 \\\\
                      &= 227  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$227  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

                      &= 227  \ \text{minutes}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many seconds are there in $21 \ \text{minutes} \text{?}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds are there in $21 \ \text{minutes} \text{?}$ 
</code></pre>

                         &= 1260 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jacqueline needs to catch a flight early tomorrow morning. She needs to get out of bed at $3.15 \ \text{am}$, but wants to make sure that she has at least $7\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text{hours}$ of sleep. What is the
                                
latest time that Jacqueline can go to sleep this evening? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jacqueline needs to catch a flight early tomorrow morning. She needs to get out of bed at $3.15 \ \text{am}$, but wants to make sure that she has at least $7\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text{hours}$ of sleep. What is the
<emptyline>
latest time that Jacqueline can go to sleep this evening? 
</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

An aeroplane leaves Heathrow at $21{:}26$ and arrives at Atlanta at $10{:}45$ on the following day. How long does this journey take, in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">An aeroplane leaves Heathrow at $21{:}26$ and arrives at Atlanta at $10{:}45$ on the following day. How long does this journey take, in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 10{:}45 - 21{:}26 \\\\
                            &= 13 \ \text{hours} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$13 \ \text{hours} \ 19 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

In fact, the times were given  as local times. The time in Atlanta is five hours behind U.K. time. What is the actual length of the journey?

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For how long was I watching television? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For how long was I watching television? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 6{:}55 \ \text{am} \\\\
&= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 40 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} + 40  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 120 + 40 \\\\
&= 160  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$160  \ \text{minutes}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9{:}35 \ \text{am} - 6{:}55 \ \text{am} \\\\
&= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 40 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
&= 2 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} + 40  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 120 + 40 \\\\
&= 160  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In total, a fifth of the time I spent watching television advertisements were being shown. What was the total time of all the advertisements? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In total, a fifth of the time I spent watching television advertisements were being shown. What was the total time of all the advertisements? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time}                           &= \dfrac {1} {5} \ \text{of} \ 160 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {32 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 32} {\cancel 5} \\\\  
                                            &= 32 \ \text {minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$32 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

                                            &= \dfrac {32 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 32} {\cancel 5} \\\\  
                                            &= 32 \ \text {minutes}

$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I start watching a film at $7{:}45 \ \text{pm}$ and turn the television off at $9{:}35 \ \text{pm}$. For how long was I watching television?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I start watching a film at $7{:}45 \ \text{pm}$ and turn the television off at $9{:}35 \ \text{pm}$. For how long was I watching television?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Watching Time} &= 9{:}35 - 7{:}45\\\\
                     &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 50 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                     &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 50  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                     &= 60 + 50 \\\\
                     &= 110  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$110  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If he is paid $\pounds 6.00$ for each hour worked, how much will he be paid for all the work?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If he is paid $\pounds 6.00$ for each hour worked, how much will he be paid for all the work?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total amount} &= 4 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{ minutes} \times 6 \\\\
                     &= (4 \ \text{hours} +   15 \ \text{ minutes} ) \times 6 \\\\
                     &= (4 \ \text{hours} +   \dfrac {15} {60} \ \text{hours} ) \times 6 \\\\
                     &= 4 \times 6 + \dfrac {15 \times 6} {60} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 6} {5 \times 2 \times 6} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 3 \times \cancel 6} {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 6} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + 1.5 \\\\ 
                     &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 25.5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

                     &= (4 \ \text{hours} +   15 \ \text{ minutes} ) \times 6 \\\\
                     &= (4 \ \text{hours} +   \dfrac {15} {60} \ \text{hours} ) \times 6 \\\\
                     &= 4 \times 6 + \dfrac {15 \times 6} {60} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 6} {5 \times 2 \times 6} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 3 \times \cancel 6} {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 6} \\\\
                     &= 24 + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
                     &= 24 + 1.5 \\\\ 
                     &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many seconds are there in a day?

- $3600$
- $1400$
- $1440$
- $51{,}840$
- $86{,}400$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds are there in a day?
<emptyline>
- $3600$
- $1400$
- $1440$
- $51{,}840$
- $86{,}400$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400  \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

**Question**

What is the time of the latest train that someone could catch from St Albans in order to arrive at 
City no later than eight o'clock?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

The $07{:}55$ train start at St Albans at $07{:}28$.

To reach the City no later than eight o'clock, someone should catch from St Albans the train at $07{:}28$. 
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Roweena lives in St Albans and needs to arrive at St Pancras in order to catch the $0855$ train to Paris. 
There is a $30 \ \text{minute}$ check-in at St Pancras International station and she allows herself an extra
 $10 \ \text{minutes}$ 
to change platforms. 
Roweena knows that it takes her $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk from her house to get to the platform at St Albans station. 
What is the latest time that she needs to leave her house?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Roweena lives in St Albans and needs to arrive at St Pancras in order to catch the $0855$ train to Paris. 
There is a $30 \ \text{minute}$ check-in at St Pancras International station and she allows herself an extra
 $10 \ \text{minutes}$ 
to change platforms. 
Roweena knows that it takes her $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk from her house to get to the platform at St Albans station. 
What is the latest time that she needs to leave her house?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Arrival time at  St Pancras International station }    & = 08{:}55 - 30 \ \text{min} - 10 \ \text{min} \\\\
                                                             & = 08{:}15
\end{aligned}
$

Roweena should reach St Pancras with a train reaching before $08{:}15$.

The train reaching St Pancras  before $0815$ is the $08{:}12$ train, starting at St Albans at $07{:}44$. 

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Latest time she needs to leave}              & = 07{:}44 - 25 \ \text{min} \\\\
                                                   & = 07{:}19
\end{aligned}
$

The latest time that Roweena need to leave her house is $= 07{:}19$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$07{:}19$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If March $23 ^ \text{rd}$ is a Monday, on what day of the week is April $2 ^ \text{nd}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If March $23 ^ \text{rd}$ is a Monday, on what day of the week is April $2 ^ \text{nd}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text {March} \ 23\text{rd} \ \text {Monday} + 7 \ \text {days} + 3 \ \text{days} \\\\
&= \text {March} \ 30\text{th} \ \text {Monday} + 3 \ \text {days} \\\\
&= \text {April} \ 2\text{nd} \ \text {Thursday}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text {Thursday}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

&= \text {March} \ 30\text{th} \ \text {Monday} + 3 \ \text {days} \\\\
&= \text {April} \ 2\text{nd} \ \text {Thursday}

</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The Swiss transportation system is the best in the world. Not only does it run on time but it is possible to transfer seamlessly from one mode of transport to another.

The journey from Giessbach to Grutschalp has three stages: a steamer across Lake Brienzersee to Interlaken, a train from Interlaken to Lauterbrunnen and finally a cable car from Lauterbrunnen to Grutschalp.

-  Steamer boats leave Giessbach every $70 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey across the lake takes $60 \ \text{minutes}$.
-  Trains leave Interlaken every $40 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $30 \ \text{minutes}$.  
-  Cable cars leave Lauterbrunnen every $15 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$.

The first steamer, train and cable car of the day are all at $07{:}00$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The Swiss transportation system is the best in the world. Not only does it run on time but it is possible to transfer seamlessly from one mode of transport to another.

<emptyline>
-  Steamer boats leave Giessbach every $70 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey across the lake takes $60 \ \text{minutes}$.
-  Trains leave Interlaken every $40 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $30 \ \text{minutes}$.  
-  Cable cars leave Lauterbrunnen every $15 \ \text{minutes}$ and the journey takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>
The first steamer, train and cable car of the day are all at $07{:}00$.
</code></pre>
</div>

**Question**

Draw the hour and minute hands on this clock to show the time $22{:}30$.

![papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2017/sample-paper-8/s01-q10-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2017/sample-paper-8/s01-q10-i00.png "papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2017/sample-paper-8/s01-q10-i00.png")

</div>

<emptyline>

**Question**

Tom has arranged to meet Nic at St. Peters Street in St. Albans at $8{:}30 \ \text{am}$.

Tom lives in Rickmansworth and is planning to travel by bus. Tom thinks that it is a Hertfordshire school holiday and arrives at the bus stop at Rickmansworth just in time to catch the bus. Unfortunately, it is a Hertfordshire school day.

How late does Tom arrive for his meeting with Nic?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
How late does Tom arrive for his meeting with Nic?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}     &= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
                      &= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 720 + 12 \\\\
                      &= 732  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$732  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

\text{Difference}     &= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
                      &= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 720 + 12 \\\\
                      &= 732  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The second hand of a clock points to the number $2$. After one minute and twenty seconds what number will it be pointing to?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The second hand of a clock points to the number $2$. After one minute and twenty seconds what number will it be pointing to?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{After one minute and twenty seconds} &= 2 + 1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {20} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {5 \times 4} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 4} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 4 \\\\
                                           &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{After one minute and twenty seconds} &= 2 + 1 \ \text{minute} \ 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 20 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {20} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {5 \times 4} {5} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 4} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                           &= 2 + 4 \\\\
                                           &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Nani flies directly to Athens from London, departing at $08{:}20 \ \text{hours}$. 
If the journey from London takes $3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes}$, at what time, local to Athens, will Nani arrive in Athens?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Nani flies directly to Athens from London, departing at $08{:}20 \ \text{hours}$. 
If the journey from London takes $3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes}$, at what time, local to Athens, will Nani arrive in Athens?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time to Athens}               &= 08{:}20 + 3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} + 2 \ \text{hours } \\\\
                                    &= 08{:}20 + 5 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 14{:}05
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14{:}05$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

                                    &= 08{:}20 + 5 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 14{:}05
\end{aligned}
$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

It takes the same time to fly from Athens to London as it takes from London to Athens.

Iani leaves Athens airport at $19{:}15 \ \text{hours}$.

At what time will she reach London (local time)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">It takes the same time to fly from Athens to London as it takes from London to Athens.
<emptyline>
Iani leaves Athens airport at $19{:}15 \ \text{hours}$.
<emptyline>
At what time will she reach London (local time)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Nani arrives in London}     &= 19{:}15 + 3 \ \text{hours } 45 \ \text{minutes} - 2 \ \text{hours } \\\\
                                    &= 19{:}15 + 1 \ \text{hour } 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 21{:}00
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$21{:}00$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q18-i00.png")

Clock $A$ is $12 \ \text{minutes}$ fast.

How many minutes slow is clock $B$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
Clock $A$ is $12 \ \text{minutes}$ fast.
<emptyline>
How many minutes slow is clock $B$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Real Time}     &= 07{:}05 - 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                     &= 06{:}53 \\\\
\text{Minutes slow}  &= 06{:}53 - 06{:}35 \\\\
                     &= 18 \ \text{minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$ 18 \ \text{minutes} $

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kyra parks her car at $10.30 \ \text{am}$. 
She collects the car at $2.15 \ \text{pm}$. 
How much does she have to pay?

| **Car Park Charge** |                  |
|:--------------------:|:----------------:|
|       **Time**       |    **Charge**    |
|     Up to $1$ hour     | $20  \text{p}$  |
|     $1$ to $2$ hours     |  $50  \text{p}$ |
|     $2$ to $3$ hours     | $\pounds  1.00$ |
|     $3$ to $4$ hours     | $\pounds  1.70$ |
|     Over $4$ hours     | $\pounds  5.00$ |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Kyra parks her car at $10.30 \ \text{am}$. 
She collects the car at $2.15 \ \text{pm}$. 
How much does she have to pay?
<emptyline>
| **Car Park Charge** |                  |
|:--------------------:|:----------------:|
|       **Time**       |    **Charge**    |
|     Up to $1$ hour     | $20  \text{p}$  |
|     $1$ to $2$ hours     |  $50  \text{p}$ |
|     $2$ to $3$ hours     | $\pounds  1.00$ |
|     $3$ to $4$ hours     | $\pounds  1.70$ |
|     Over $4$ hours     | $\pounds  5.00$ |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>

\text{Parking Time}         &= 02{:}15 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30  \ \text{am}  \\\\
                            &= 3 \ \text{hours} \ 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Parking charge}       &= \pounds 1.70
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

$\pounds 1.70$

</div>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The time it takes to get from Bunley to Wester is always the 
same. What time did I depart from Bunley if I arrive in Wester at $12 \ 14?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken from Bunley to Wester }      &= 10{:}32 - 08{:}00 \\\\
                                              &=  2 \ \text{hours} \ 32 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Time of depart from Bunley}             &= 12{:}14 -  2 \ \text{hours} \ 32 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                                              &= 09{:}42
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$09{:}42$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Helena went on a journey.

She spent $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of her total travelling time in 
the car. She then spent $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of her remaining travelling 
time on the train. She spent $2 \ \text{hours}$ on the train. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long did she spend in a car?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long did she spend in a car?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let total travelling time $= a$

Time spent travelling in a car $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text{of} \ (a - \dfrac {a} {2})     &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {1} {2} \times \ (\dfrac{a \times 2}{1 \times 2} - \dfrac {a} {2})     &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {1} {2} \times \ (\dfrac{2a}{2} - \dfrac {a} {2})     &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {1} {2} \times \ (\dfrac{2a - a} {2})     &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {a} {2}                  &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {a} {2 \times 2}                               &=  2 \\\\ 
\dfrac {a} {4}                                        &=  2 \\\\ 
a                                                     &= 2 \times 4 \\\\
a                                                     &= 8  \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total travelling time}                          &=  8  \ \text{hours} \\\\
\text{Time spent in a car}                            &=  \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
                                                      &=  \dfrac {8} {2} \\\\
                                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                                                      &= 4 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$ 4 \ \text{hours}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The start and finish times of a film are shown on this notice. 

| **The Tiger Prince** |                     |
|:--------------------:|:-------------------:|
|         Start        |  $7.45 \ \text{pm}$ |
|          End         | $10.34 \ \text{pm}$ |

For how long did the film last?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

<emptyline>
| **The Tiger Prince** |                     |
|:--------------------:|:-------------------:|
|         Start        |  $7.45 \ \text{pm}$ |
|          End         | $10.34 \ \text{pm}$ |
<emptyline>
For how long did the film last?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Film lasted} &= 10{:}34 - 7{:}45  \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 49 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

**Question**

When it is $16.42$ in London, what time is it in New York?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

                        &= 11{:}42
\end{aligned}
$

**Question**

An aeroplane leaves Addis Ababa at $19.00$ on Tuesday and travels to New York.

The whole journey takes $14 \ \text{hours}$.

At what time and on which day does it arrive in New York?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
The whole journey takes $14 \ \text{hours}$.
<emptyline>
At what time and on which day does it arrive in New York?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival Time} &= 19{:}00 + 14 \ \text{hours} - 7 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                    &= 19{:}00 + 7  \ \text{hours}\\\\
                    &= 2{:}00 \\\\
\text {Day of Arrival} &= \text {Wednesday}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Time $=$ $2{:}00$

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Day $=$ Wednesday

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The instructions below are used to work out the cooking time for a turkey.

$50 \ \text{minutes per kilogram}$ plus $20 \ \text{minutes}$

Claire wants to cook a turkey with a mass of $2.5 \ \text{kilograms}$.

For how long should Claire cook the turkey?

*Give your answer in hours and minutes.*

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The instructions below are used to work out the cooking time for a turkey.
<emptyline>
$50 \ \text{minutes per kilogram}$ plus $20 \ \text{minutes}$
<emptyline>
Claire wants to cook a turkey with a mass of $2.5 \ \text{kilograms}$.
<emptyline>
For how long should Claire cook the turkey?
<emptyline>
*Give your answer in hours and minutes.*
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cooking Time}         &= 2.5 \times 50 \ \text {minutes} + 20 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 125 + 20 \\\\
                             &= 145 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 120 \ \text {minutes} + 25 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 25 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text {hours} \ 25 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

                             &= 125 + 20 \\\\
                             &= 145 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 120 \ \text {minutes} + 25 \ \text {minutes} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The school concert starts at $2{:}45 \ \text{p.m}$.

It lasts for $2 \ \text{hours}$ and $28 \ \text{minutes}$ and, in addition, there is an interval of $20 \ \text{minutes}$.

At what time does the concert end?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The school concert starts at $2{:}45 \ \text{p.m}$.
<emptyline>
It lasts for $2 \ \text{hours}$ and $28 \ \text{minutes}$ and, in addition, there is an interval of $20 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>
At what time does the concert end?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
&= 2 {:} 45 \ \text {pm} + 2 \ \text {hours} \ 28 \ \text {minutes} + 20 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 2 {:} 45 \ \text {pm} + 2 \ \text {hours} \ 48 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 5 {:} 33 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 {:} 33 \ \text {pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned} 
&= 2 {:} 45 \ \text {pm} + 2 \ \text {hours} \ 28 \ \text {minutes} + 20 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 2 {:} 45 \ \text {pm} + 2 \ \text {hours} \ 48 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 5 {:} 33 \ \text {pm}

**Question**

For how much longer did Rebecca remain silent than Holly?

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If each girl was sponsored $10 \ \text{pence}$ for each minute that she remained silent, how much money did the three girls raise in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If each girl was sponsored $10 \ \text{pence}$ for each minute that she remained silent, how much money did the three girls raise in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Silent Time}                  &= 16{:}29 - 15{:}00 + 17{:}08 - 15{:}00 + 16{:}23 - 15{:}00 \\\\
                                     &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 29 \ \text {minutes} + 2 \ \text {hour} \ 8 \ \text {minutes} + 1 \ \text {hour} \ 23 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                     &= 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 29 \ \text {minutes} + 2 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 8 \ \text {minutes} + 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 23 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                     &= 60 + 29 + 120 + 8 + 60 + 23 \\\\
                                     &= 300 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
\text {Total money raised}           &= 300 \times 10 \ \text {pence} \\\\
                                     &= 3000 \ \text {pence}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

$3000 \ \text {pence}$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What time is it in London when it is $1.15 \ \text{p.m.}$ in Sri Lanka?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The time in New York is $5 \ \text{hours}$ behind the time in London.

Bill's journey from New York to Sri Lanka takes exactly $30 \ \text{hours}$.

Bill leaves New York on Wednesday at $19{:}40$

On which day and at what time does Bill arrive in Sri Lanka?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The time in New York is $5 \ \text{hours}$ behind the time in London.
<emptyline>
Bill's journey from New York to Sri Lanka takes exactly $30 \ \text{hours}$.
<emptyline>
Bill leaves New York on Wednesday at $19{:}40$
<emptyline>
On which day and at what time does Bill arrive in Sri Lanka?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time in Sri Lanka}  &= 19{:}40 + 30 \ \text{hours} +  4 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text{hours}+ 5 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 19{:}40 + 30 \ \text{hours} +   4 \ \text{hours} \ 30 \ \text{minutes} + 5 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 19{:}40 + 30 \ \text{hours} +   4 \ \text{hours} + 30 \ \text{minutes} + 5 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                           &= 19{:}40 + 39 \ \text{hours} +   30 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                           &= 19{:}40 + 1 \ \text{day} + 15 \ \text{hours} +   30 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                           &= 19{:}40  + 15 \ \text{hours} \ 30 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                           &= 11{:}10
\end{aligned}
$

Day Bill arrived Sri Lanka $= \text {Friday}$
</div>
</div>

**Answer**

$11{:}10$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Katy is going to the cinema to see a film that starts at $3.25 \ \text{pm}$ and lasts $108 \ \text{minutes}$.

At what time will the film end?

Write your answer using the $24{-}\text{hour}$ clock.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Katy is going to the cinema to see a film that starts at $3.25 \ \text{pm}$ and lasts $108 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>
At what time will the film end?
<emptyline>
Write your answer using the $24{-}\text{hour}$ clock.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Ending time}     &= 3{:}25 + 108 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                       &= 15{:}25 + 108 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                       &= 15{:}25 + 1 \ \text{hour} \ 48 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                       &= 17{:}13
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17{:}13$

</div>
</div>

Train $1$ takes $57 \ \text{minutes}$ to get from Spongeton to Squareford.

Each train takes the same amount of time to travel from Bobville to Pantsbridge.

Train $2$ takes twice as long to travel from Bobville to Squareford as from Spongeton to Bobville.

\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by train} \ 3  &= 13{:}45 - 12{:}15 \\\\
                                &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 30 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

$ \text{Train} \ 3 \ \text{takes the longest}$.

**Question**

Assuming that no time is added to the journey when a train passes through a station, 
work out the route that takes the shortest time to travel from $A$ to $G$.

You should list the stations in order.

Your route **does not** need to pass through every station.

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sherry's train to Bristol was scheduled to leave at $13{:}40$ and to arrive at $14{:}20$

However, the train left eight minutes late and then took $47 \ \text{minutes}$.

At what time did Sherry arrive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sherry's train to Bristol was scheduled to leave at $13{:}40$ and to arrive at $14{:}20$
<emptyline>
However, the train left eight minutes late and then took $47 \ \text{minutes}$.
<emptyline>
At what time did Sherry arrive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival time} &= 13{:}40 + 8 \ \text {minutes} + 47 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 13{:}40 + 55 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 14{:}35
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14{:}35$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which bus takes the shortest time from Elgin to Inverness and by how many minutes?

|           |          Bus P          |          Bus Q          |
|:---------:|:-----------------------:|:-----------------------:|
|   Elgin   | $11{:}47 \ \text{a.m.}$ | $12{:}33 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
| Inverness |  $1{:}06 \ \text{p.m.}$ |  $1{:}50 \ \text{p.m.}$ |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which bus takes the shortest time from Elgin to Inverness and by how many minutes?
<emptyline>
|           |          Bus P          |          Bus Q          |
|:---------:|:-----------------------:|:-----------------------:|
|   Elgin   | $11{:}47 \ \text{a.m.}$ | $12{:}33 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
| Inverness |  $1{:}06 \ \text{p.m.}$ |  $1{:}50 \ \text{p.m.}$ |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                   &= (1.06 - 11.47) - (1.50 - 12.33)  \\\\
                                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{hour} \ 17 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 2 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text {Bus Q}$ $=$ $2 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

                                    &= 2 \ \text{minutes}

**Question**

Amira spends five minutes at the shop before walking another twenty-three minutes to get to school.

At what time does Amira reach school?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Amira spends five minutes at the shop before walking another twenty-three minutes to get to school.
<emptyline>

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrival Time} &= 7{:}40 + 5 \ \text {minutes} + 23 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 7{:}40 + 28 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                    &= 8{:}08 \ \text {am}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

**Question**

The table shows part of the railway timetable for trains from Andforth to Ermston. 

Some trains stop at stations between, and others are express trains. 

All stopping trains take the same time between stations as each other.

|          | Express   | Stopping   | Express   | Stopping   | Express   | Stopping |
|----------|:---------:|------------|-----------|------------|-----------|----------|
| Andforth | $13{:}18$ | $13{:}43$  | $14{:}24$ | $14{:}41$  | $15{:}20$ | $15{:}34$|
| Bowlton  |           | $14{:}06$  |           | $15{:}04$  |           |          |
| Cheeble  |           | $14{:}18$  |           | $15{:}16$  |           |          |
| Delf     |           | $14{:}23$  |           | $15{:}21$  |           |          |
| Ermston  | $14{:}02$ | $14{:}38$  | $15{:}08$ | $15{:}36$  | $16{:}04$ |          |

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much longer does the stopping train take than the express for the journey from Andforth to Ermston?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much longer does the stopping train take than the express for the journey from Andforth to Ermston?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}           &= \text{Time taken by stopping train} - \text{Time taken by express train} \\\\
                            &= (14{:}38 - 13{:}43) - (14{:}02 - 13{:}18) \\\\
                            &=  55 \ \text{minutes}  - 44 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 11  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
