<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The stopping trains travel at an average speed of $36 \ \text{miles per hour}$ for the whole journey. Work out the distance from Andforth to Ermston?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The stopping trains travel at an average speed of $36 \ \text{miles per hour}$ for the whole journey. Work out the distance from Andforth to Ermston?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}           &= \text{Speed} \times \text{Time} \\\\
                          &= 36 \times (14{:}38 - 13{:}43) \\\\
                          &= 36 \times (55 \ \text{minutes}) \\\\
                          &= 36 \times \dfrac {55} {60} \\\\
                          &=  \dfrac {36 \times 55} {60} \\\\
                          &=  \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times 3 \times 5 \times 11} {6 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
                          &=  \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times \cancel 2 \times 3 \times \cancel 5 \times 11} {\cancel 6 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
                          &= 3 \times 11 \\\\
                          &= 33 \ \text{miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$33 \ \text{miles}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the average speed of the express trains in miles per hour.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the average speed of the express trains in miles per hour.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Average Speed of express train}   &= \dfrac {\text{Distance between Andforth and Ermston}}  {\text{Time taken to travel from Andforth to Ermston}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {33}  {14{:}02 - 13{:}18} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {33}  {14{:}02 - 13{:}18} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {33}  { \dfrac {44} {60} } \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {33 \times 60 }  {44} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {3 \times 11 \times 4 \times 15 }  {4 \times 11} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{11} \times \cancel 4 \times 15 }  {\cancel 4 \times \cancel{11}} \\\\
                       &= 3 \times 15 \\\\
                       &= 45 \ \text{miles per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45 \ \text{miles per hour}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Neil is allowed to watch three hours of TV each day in the holidays. 
He watches cartoons from $6{:}20 \ \text{pm}$ to $7{:}10 \ \text{pm}$ and a 
sports programme from $7{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ to $9{:}00 \ \text{pm}$. 

For how much longer could he have watched TV without breaking his rule?  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

sports programme from $7{:}25 \ \text{pm}$ to $9{:}00 \ \text{pm}$. 
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>

ttime

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alison watches two quiz programmes on TV. The first 
is on from $6{:}35 \ \text{pm}$ until $7{:}15 \ \text{pm}$, the 
second lasts for half an hour. Later on, she watches 
a film from $9{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ until $11{:}25 \ \text{pm}$. In 
hours and minutes, how long has she spent watching TV?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alison watches two quiz programmes on TV. The first 
is on from $6{:}35 \ \text{pm}$ until $7{:}15 \ \text{pm}$, the 
second lasts for half an hour. Later on, she watches 
a film from $9{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ until $11{:}25 \ \text{pm}$. In 
hours and minutes, how long has she spent watching TV?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time spent}     &= 7{:}15 - 6{:}35 + 30 \ \text{minutes}  + 11{:}25  - 9{:}30 \\\\
                            &= 40 \ \text{minutes} + 30 \ \text{minutes} + 1 \ \text{hour} \ 55 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 40 \ \text{minutes} + 30 \ \text{minutes} + 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} +  55 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 40 + 30 + 60 + 55 \\\\
                            &= 185 \ \text{minutes} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$185 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What is the latest time he should leave Cambridge to catch his flight?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

                              &= 10{:}47 - 2 \ \text{hours} \ 50 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                              &= 10{:}47 - 02{:}50 \\\\
                              &= 07{:}57 \text{ am}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Frank catches the $7{:}52$ train from Cambridge to Northampton and he arrives in Northampton at $9{:}21$. How long was is journey? Give your answer in hours and minutes.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Frank catches the $7{:}52$ train from Cambridge to Northampton and he arrives in Northampton at $9{:}21$. How long was is journey? Give your answer in hours and minutes.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Duration} &= 9{:}21 - 7{:}52 \\\\
                 &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 29 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

**Question**

My train was $17\ \text{minutes}$ late when it arrived at the station at $17{:}05$.


What time was it due to arrive at the station?

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q13-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q13-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q13-i00.png")

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My train was $17\ \text{minutes}$ late when it arrived at the station at $17{:}05$.

<emptyline>

</code></pre>

                                     &= 16{:}48
\end{aligned}
$

**Question**

How many minutes are there between $9{:}23 \ \text{am}$ and $1{:}06 \ \text{pm}$? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train leaves Manchester and arrives in London at $13{:}37$ 

What time did the train depart Manchester if the total journey time was $173$ $\text{minutes}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train leaves Manchester and arrives in London at $13{:}37$ 
<emptyline>
What time did the train depart Manchester if the total journey time was $173$ $\text{minutes}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Departure time} &= 13{:}37 - 173 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                       &= 13{:}37 - 2 \ \text {hours } 53 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                       &= 10{:}44
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10{:}44$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A plane leaves London Airport at $16{:}30$ and takes $5\dfrac {3} {4}\ \text{hours}$ on its journey. At what time does it arrive?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A plane leaves London Airport at $16{:}30$ and takes $5\dfrac {3} {4}\ \text{hours}$ on its journey. At what time does it arrive?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Arrival time} &= 16{:}30 + 5 \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {hours} \\\\
                     &= 16{:}30 + 5 \ \text {hours } 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                     &= 22{:}15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$22{:}15$

</div>
</div>
